// Monster command scripts, round 7 (src/game/unk_722400.c, unk_72E450.c). EmScript_Run interprets the
// monster's current script: it reads a command byte and calls the command's handler with the bytes after
// it, handler(em, p + 1), which returns the next command (at most 1000 commands a frame). The handlers
// are named by their command byte, EmScript_OpNN (ops 0x04 Em_StartScript, 0x07 EmScript_Start and 0x16
// EmScript_Call were named before; 0x7B and 0x84 share EmScript_Op7B). Many are "if" commands: their
// first byte is 0 (if: when the condition fails, skip to the op's else (op, 1) or end (op, 2)), 1 (else:
// skip to the end) or 2 (end). EmScript_NextCmd(em, p) steps over the command at p (a switch on every
// op's length), EmScript_SkipBlock(em, p, op, 2) over one command and any nested block of the same op,
// EmScript_SkipToEnd(em, p, op) to past the op's end.

EmScript_SkipBlock = 0x00731CA0; // type:func
EmScript_NextCmd = 0x00731DA0; // type:func
EmScript_SkipToEnd = 0x00733150; // type:func
